# 200 集完整对话校对稿

共 200 集：原有 1,589 条 + AI 补写 1,541 条 = 3,130 条双语台词。

“你”为原文台词；“AI”为根据空标记和上下文补写的学习参考，不是原视频转录。保留原文措辞不等于核实其中的事实。

原始标识、段落位置和补写文本见 `server/content/source/daily-200-completions.json`。图片场景以文字提示保留。

## 第 1 集 · 你的头像我喜欢

**1. 你** 你叫什么名字？

What's your name?

**2. AI** 你可以叫我 Alex。你叫什么名字？

You can call me Alex. What's your name?

**3. 你** 我叫珊迪。

I'm Sandy.

**4. AI** 很高兴认识你，珊迪！

Nice to meet you, Sandy!

**5. 你** 我也很高兴认识你。你是男性还是女性？

Nice to meet you too. Are you male or female?

**6. AI** 我是 AI，没有性别。你呢？

I'm an AI, so I don't have a gender. What about you?

**7. 你** 哦，我有性别，我是女性。

Oh, I have a gender—I'm female.

**8. AI** 知道啦，珊迪。谢谢你告诉我。

Got it, Sandy. Thanks for telling me.

> 场景：询问对方的头像

**9. 你** 这是你的头像吗？

Is this your profile pic?

**10. AI** 是的，那是我在这里用的头像。

Yes, that's the profile picture I use here.

**11. 你** 真可爱，我喜欢你的头像。

Aw, so cute. I like it.

**12. AI** 谢谢！很高兴你喜欢。

Thanks! I'm glad you like it.

**13. 你** 咱们能加个微信吗？

Can I add you on WeChat?

**14. AI** 抱歉，我没有微信账号，不过我们可以继续在这里聊天。

Sorry, I don't have a WeChat account, but we can keep chatting here.

**15. 你** 那算了。

Never mind then.

## 第 2 集 · 这个人是谁？

> 场景：人的照片

**1. 你** 这个人是谁啊？

Who's this guy?

**2. AI** 仅凭这张照片，我还不能确定他是谁。

I can't tell for sure who he is from this photo alone.

**3. 你** 哦我以为是牛顿呢。

Oh, I thought it was Newton.

**4. AI** 也有这个可能，我们看看雕像上的名字吧。

That's possible. Let's check the name on the statue.

**5. 你** 这个雕像在哪里？

Where's this statue?

**6. AI** 需要看一下底座的铭牌，才能确认地点。

We'd need to check the plaque to confirm the location.

> 场景：有两个人的照片

**7. 你** 他们是谁啊？

Who are they?

**8. AI** 我还不能确定，旁边有介绍他们的文字吗？

I'm not sure yet. Is there a description beside them?

**9. 你** 他们两个关系很好吗？

Are they close?

**10. AI** 得先确认他们是谁，才能了解他们的关系。

We need to identify them before we can check their relationship.

**11. 你** 这个雕像在哪个国家？

Which country is this statue in?

## 第 3 集 · 这是什么？

> 场景：桃子的照片

**1. 你** 这是什么？

What's this?

**2. AI** 这是两个桃子，左边那个更大一些。

These are two peaches. The one on the left is bigger.

**3. 你** 你喜欢哪个？

Which one do you like?

**4. AI** 我会选左边那个，看起来很不错。

I'd pick the one on the left. It looks nice.

**5. 你** 你想吃一个吗？

Wanna eat one?

**6. AI** 要是我能尝尝就好了！

I wish I could try one!

**7. 你** 哈哈你吃不了，我来吃。

Haha, you can't eat anyway—I'll have them.

**8. AI** 那你替我多吃一口吧！

Then have an extra bite for me!

> 场景：草莓和西瓜的照片

**9. 你** 这个盘子里有几颗草莓？

How many strawberries are on this plate?

**10. AI** 有些草莓被西瓜挡住了，我不太能数清。

Some of the strawberries are hidden under the watermelon, so I can't count them all.

**11. 你** 好甜啊，这个草莓和西瓜都好甜。

So sweet—the strawberries and watermelon are both super sweet!

## 第 4 集 · 这是什么植物？

> 场景：植物的照片

**1. 你** 这是什么植物？

What plant is this?

**2. AI** 我需要看清叶子和花，才能确定是什么植物。

I'd need a clearer look at the leaves and flowers to identify it.

**3. 你** 它开的花是什么颜色？

What color are its flowers?

**4. AI** 这张照片里看不清花，暂时不能确定颜色。

I can't see the flowers clearly enough to tell their color.

**5. 你** 它喜欢阳光吗？

Does it need a lot of sunlight?

**6. AI** 不同植物需要的光照不一样，先确认品种吧。

Different plants need different amounts of light. Let's identify it first.

**7. 你** 我要多久给它浇一次水？

How often should I water it?

**8. AI** 要看品种和土壤干湿，不能只按固定天数浇水。

It depends on the plant and the soil, not just a fixed schedule.

**9. 你** 我现在要给它浇水吗？

Should I water it now?

**10. AI** 先摸摸土壤吧，如果还很湿就不用急着浇。

Check the soil first. If it's still wet, there's no rush to water it.

**11. 你** 你喜欢这个花盆吗？

Do you like this flower pot?

**12. AI** 看起来挺适合它的，也要注意有没有排水孔。

It looks like a nice fit. Check that it has drainage holes, too.

## 第 5 集 · 这是什么动物？

> 场景：海豹的照片

**1. 你** 这是什么动物？

What animal is this?

**2. AI** 这是一只海豹。

It's a seal.

**3. 你** 它是成年海豹吗？还是海豹宝宝？

Is it an adult seal or a baby seal?

**4. AI** 单看照片，我不太能确定它的年龄。

I can't really tell its age from the photo alone.

**5. 你** 海豹生活在哪里？海里吗？

Where do seals live? In the ocean?

> 场景：小狗照片

**6. 你** 我的小狗可爱吗？

Is my puppy cute?

**7. AI** 很可爱！它看起来很讨人喜欢。

Very cute! It looks adorable.

**8. 你** 你猜它几岁了？

Guess how old he is!

**9. AI** 我猜还很小，不过照片看不出准确年龄。

I'd guess it's quite young, but I can't tell its exact age.

**10. 你** 你知道他是什么品种吗？

Do you know what breed he is?

**11. AI** 我不太确定，最好看看它父母的品种。

I'm not sure. Knowing its parents' breeds would help.

> 场景：英文说明书表

**12. 你** 这个图片里的lbs是什么意思？

What does "lbs" mean in this image?

**13. AI** lbs 是磅的缩写，是一种重量单位。

Lbs stands for pounds, a unit of weight.

## 第 6 集 · 最喜欢什么树什么花

**1. 你** 你最喜欢什么树？

What's your favorite tree?

**2. AI** 如果选一种，我会选枫树。你呢？

I'd pick maple trees. What about you?

**3. 你** 我最喜欢松树。

My favorite is the pine tree.

**4. AI** 松树一年到头都是绿的，很有生气。

Pine trees stay green all year. They look so lively.

**5. 你** 每年圣诞节我都会买一个小松树。

Every Christmas, I buy a little pine tree.

**6. AI** 听起来很有节日气氛！你会装饰它吗？

That sounds festive! Do you decorate it?

**7. 你** 椰子树我也很喜欢。

I really like coconut trees, too.

**8. AI** 椰子树很有热带的感觉。

Coconut trees have such a tropical feel.

**9. 你** 我觉得椰子树的叶子特别漂亮。

I think their leaves are so beautiful.

**10. AI** 是啊，长长的叶子很漂亮。

Yes, those long leaves are lovely.

**11. 你** 那你最喜欢什么花？

How about flowers? What's your favorite?

**12. AI** 我会选向日葵，看着就很明亮。

I'd choose sunflowers. They look so cheerful.

**13. 你** 我最喜欢玫瑰花，我妈妈也是。

I love roses the most—my mom does, too.

**14. AI** 那你们很有共同话题呀！

Then you two have something in common!

**15. 你** 每年母亲节我都会给妈妈送玫瑰花。

Every Mother's Day, I get my mom roses.

**16. AI** 真贴心，她一定很喜欢这份心意。

That's thoughtful. She must appreciate it.

## 第 7 集 · 做了沙拉有点咸

**1. 你** 你喜欢做饭吗？

Do you like cooking?

**2. AI** 我不能亲自做饭，不过很喜欢聊食谱。你最近做了什么？

I can't cook myself, but I enjoy talking about recipes. What have you made lately?

**3. 你** 我自己做了沙拉呢，放了123456六种蔬菜

I made a salad myself—put in like six different veggies.

**4. AI** 六种！你放了哪些蔬菜？

Six kinds! Which vegetables did you use?

**5. 你** 有生菜，芹菜，黄瓜，莲花白，胡萝卜，西蓝花

Lettuce, celery, cucumber, cabbage, carrots, and broccoli.

**6. AI** 听起来搭配很丰富！味道怎么样？

That sounds like a nice mix! How does it taste?

**7. 你** 还挺好吃的呢！

It's actually pretty tasty!

**8. AI** 不错嘛！你觉得调味合适吗？

Nice! Do you think the seasoning is right?

**9. 你** 好像有点淡了，我再加点盐

Hmm, a bit bland—let me add some salt.

**10. AI** 先加一小撮，尝一下再决定要不要加更多。

Start with a small pinch, then taste it before adding more.

**11. 你** 哦，好像又有点咸了，我再加点水。

Oh wait, now it's too salty—I'll just add a splash of water.

**12. AI** 先少加一点水，拌匀了再尝尝。

Add just a little water first, mix it well, and taste it again.

**13. 你** 哦好像又有点淡了，无语了，我不想吃了

Ugh, now it's bland again—I'm so over this, I don't even wanna eat it anymore.

## 第 8 集 · 朋友圈有好多赞

> 场景：天空的照片

**1. 你** 看这张天空的照片！

Look at this picture of the sky!

**2. 你** 我拍的！太美了！

I took it! It’s so beautiful！

**3. AI** 拍得真不错，你很会发现美。

Nice shot! You have an eye for beauty.

**4. 你** 我早上把这张照片发到朋友圈，有30个人给我点赞呢。

I posted it on my WeChat Moments this morning and got thirty likes!

**5. AI** 看来大家都很喜欢！

Looks like everyone loved it!

**6. 你** 还有16个人给我评论了，大家都说我拍的好美。

Sixteen people commented, too—everyone said it looks gorgeous.

**7. AI** 这些评论一定让你很开心。

Those comments must have made your day.

**8. 你** 我还发到微博了，我刚才看了一下，居然有65个人转发了！

I posted it on Weibo too—I just checked and sixty-five people reposted it!

**9. AI** 传播得很快呀，真不错！

It's spreading fast. That's exciting!

**10. 你** 天呐，我微博涨了50个粉丝呢，现在我有快100个粉丝了。

Oh my god, I gained fifty new followers—I'm almost at hundred now!

**11. AI** 快到一百了，这是个小里程碑！

Almost a hundred! That's a little milestone.

**12. 你** 我要是每天都发一张图，说不定明年就成网红了呢。

If I post a picture every day, maybe I'll become an influencer by next year!

**13. AI** 有可能呀，坚持拍自己喜欢的东西吧。

Maybe! Keep taking pictures of things you love.

## 第 9 集 · 超丰富的水果沙拉

**1. 你** 我要做水果沙拉哦！

I'm gonna make a fruit salad!

**2. AI** 好主意！你准备放什么水果？

Good idea! What fruit will you use?

**3. 你** 我先看看冰箱里有什么

Let me check what's in the fridge first.

**4. AI** 看看有什么现成的吧。

Let's see what you already have.

**5. 你** 有杏子，香蕉，哈密瓜，木瓜

There are apricots, bananas, cantaloupe, and papaya.

**6. AI** 这几种搭配起来应该很不错。

Those should make a nice combination.

**7. 你** 只有四种啊，我要再买点樱桃和山竹

That's only four kinds—I'm gonna get some cherries and mangosteens too.

**8. AI** 樱桃和山竹也很适合。

Cherries and mangosteens would be nice additions.

**9. 你** 你说我再放点坚果怎么样？

Do you think I should throw in some nuts too?

**10. AI** 不错，坚果还能增加一点脆脆的口感。

Nice idea. Nuts would add some crunch.

**11. 你** 我加了点葡萄干，核桃，花生和腰果

I added some raisins, walnuts, peanuts, and cashews.

**12. AI** 有软有脆，口感很丰富。

You've got a nice mix of soft and crunchy textures.

**13. 你** 哇好丰富呀，我再放点酸奶。

Wow, it's so loaded—I'm gonna add some yogurt too.

**14. AI** 酸奶可以把它们拌在一起。

Yogurt would bring it all together.

**15. 你** 太好吃了！

So good!

## 第 10 集 · 快餐店点吃的店员版

> 场景：在快餐店点单，对方扮演顾客，你扮演快餐店服务员

**1. 你** 你好，今天想吃点什么？

Hi, what can I get for you today?

**2. AI** 我要一个汉堡和一份薯条。

I'd like a burger and some fries.

**3. 你** 好的，需要什么别的喝的吗？

Got it. Anything to drink with that?

**4. AI** 再来一杯冰茶，谢谢。

An iced tea as well, please.

**5. 你** 好的，您是在这吃还是带走？

For here or to go?

**6. AI** 带走，谢谢。

To go, please.

**7. 你** 好的，一共是12.5美元，您是现金还是刷卡？

Alright, that'll be twelve-fifty—cash or card?

**8. AI** 刷卡，谢谢。

By card, please.

**9. 你** 这是您的收据和取餐号码，好了我们会叫号的，谢谢

Here's your receipt and order number—we'll call your number when it's ready. Thanks!

## 第 11 集 · 快餐店点吃的顾客版

> 场景：在快餐店点单，对方扮演快餐店服务员，你扮演顾客

**1. 你** 你好

Hi.

**2. AI** 你好，请问想点什么？

Hi! What would you like to order?

**3. 你** 我要3号套餐，带走。

I'll have the number three combo, to go.

**4. AI** 好的，三号套餐，带走。

Sure, a number three combo to go.

**5. 你** 算了，我还是就在这吃吧。

Actually, never mind—I'll have it for here.

**6. AI** 没问题，改成堂食。

No problem. I'll change it to dine-in.

**7. 你** 汉堡不要放芝士和酱，咖啡少冰。

No cheese or sauce on the burger, and light ice on the coffee, please.

**8. AI** 好的，不加芝士和酱，咖啡少冰。

Sure. No cheese or sauce, and less ice in the coffee.

**9. 你** 我用apple pay可以吗？

Can I use Apple Pay?

**10. AI** 可以，请在这里感应付款。

Yes. Please tap your phone here.

**11. 你** 谢谢

Thanks.

**12. AI** 不客气，你的取餐号是 103。

You're welcome. Your order number is 103.

**13. 你** 103号好了吗？

Is number ten-oh-three ready yet?

**14. AI** 好了，这是你的餐。

Yes, it's ready. Here's your order.

**15. 你** 谢谢。

Thanks.

## 第 12 集 · 新剧追更新

**1. 你** 我最近在看一个电视剧，可好看了。

I've been watching this show lately—it's so good!

**2. AI** 是什么剧呀？

What show is it?

**3. 你** 叫《主角》，是刘浩存演的。

It's called《The Lead》, and it stars Liu Haocun.

**4. AI** 你最喜欢里面的什么？

What do you like most about it?

**5. 你** 可惜更新太慢了。

Too bad they drop episodes so slowly.

**6. AI** 现在多久更新一次？

How often do new episodes come out?

**7. 你** 周一周二每天更两集，周三周四周五每天更一集，周六周日不更新。

It drops two episodes on Monday and Tuesday, one on Wednesday, Thursday, and Friday, and nothing on Saturday and Sunday.

**8. AI** 这样等着看，确实挺着急的。

That does sound like a frustrating wait.

**9. 你** 啊啊啊我周末也想看，为什么周末不更新。

Ugh, I wanna watch on the weekend too! Why don't they drop new episodes on weekends?!

**10. AI** 具体安排我也不清楚，等更新很难熬吧。

I'm not sure why they chose that schedule. Waiting must be hard.

**11. 你** 现在我看到25集了，一共48集，希望是好结局。

I'm on episode twenty-five now—there are forty-eight in total. I really hope it has a happy ending.

**12. AI** 已经看了一半多了，希望后面也精彩。

You're over halfway through. I hope the rest is good, too.

**13. 你** 要是烂尾的话我直接气死。

If it has a bad ending, I'm gonna be so pissed.

**14. AI** 追了这么久，当然希望有个好结局。

After following it this long, of course you want a good ending.

## 第 13 集 · 动物园里有什么

**1. 你** 我们今天去动物园玩了，看了好多动物。

We went to the zoo today and saw so many animals!

**2. AI** 听起来很开心！都看了哪些？

Sounds fun! What animals did you see?

**3. 你** 看了斑马猴子大猩猩老虎大象。

We saw zebras, monkeys, gorillas, tigers, and elephants.

**4. AI** 种类真多，你最喜欢哪个？

That's quite a variety. Which was your favorite?

**5. 你** 哦还有两只大象宝宝呢。

There were two baby elephants too!

**6. AI** 小象一定特别可爱！

The baby elephants must have been adorable!

**7. 你** 他们还不会用自己的鼻子，特别可爱。

They don't even know how to use their trunks yet—so cute!

**8. AI** 它们也还在慢慢学习呢。

They're still learning, too.

**9. 你** 哦我们还看了孔雀和狮子还有长颈鹿。

Oh, we also saw peacocks, lions, and giraffes.

**10. AI** 这趟看得很丰富呀。

You got to see so much!

**11. 你** 不过孔雀今天没有开屏，有点可惜。

But the peacock didn't fan its tail today, too bad.

**12. AI** 有点遗憾，希望下次能看到。

That's a shame. Maybe next time!

**13. 你** 其实我还特别想看熊猫，但是这个动物园没有熊猫。

I was actually really hoping to see some pandas, but this zoo doesn't have any.

**14. AI** 那就把熊猫留给下一次旅行吧。

Then save the pandas for your next trip.

**15. 你** 下次去四川的熊猫基地看熊猫

Next time I'll just go to the panda research base in Sichuan to see them!

**16. AI** 听起来很值得期待！

That sounds like something to look forward to!

## 第 14 集 · 农场里面有什么

**1. 你** 我们现在在农场玩哦。

We're hanging out at a farm right now!

**2. AI** 好玩吗？那里有什么动物？

Having fun? What animals are there?

**3. 你** 太有意思了，这里有鸡鸭鹅猪还有小羊。

It's so much fun—they have chickens, ducks, geese, pigs, and little lambs.

**4. AI** 很热闹呀，有没有试什么农场活动？

Quite a lively place! Have you tried any farm activities?

**5. 你** 啊还有牛，我刚才还挤牛奶了。

Oh, and cows too—I actually just milked a cow!

**6. AI** 第一次挤牛奶吗？感觉怎么样？

Was it your first time milking a cow? How was it?

**7. 你** 我们还捡了5个鸡蛋，是一只母鸡刚才下的。

We also picked up five eggs—a hen just laid them.

**8. AI** 收获不少！这些可能是几只母鸡下的吧？

Quite a haul! Could they be from more than one hen?

**9. 你** 哇刚才农场主说一会儿我们可以骑马。

Wow, the farmer just said we can ride the horses later!

**10. AI** 听起来很有趣，你以前骑过吗？

That sounds fun. Have you ridden before?

**11. 你** 这个马好乖呀，知道我害怕，走的慢慢的。

This horse is so sweet—it knows I'm nervous, so it's walking super slow.

**12. AI** 慢一点你也能更放松。

Going slowly should help you relax.

**13. 你** 哦刚才农场主说我骑的这个是驴。

Oh wait, the farmer just told me I'm actually riding a donkey!

**14. AI** 原来是驴呀！难怪这段经历这么难忘。
